We intentionally only match <hp:t>...</hp:t> (no attributes) because\n# templates typically use this form; if attributes appear, we can widen later.\nT_RE = re.compile(r\"(<hp:t>)(.*?)(</hp:t>)\", re.DOTALL)\n\n# Layout cache element (may be hp:linesegarray or linesegarray).\nLINESEG_RE = re.compile(\n    r\"<(?:hp:)?linesegarray\\b[^>]*>.*?</(?:hp:)?linesegarray>\",\n    re.DOTALL,\n)\n\n\ndef _xml_escape_text(s: str) -> str:\n    # Minimal escaping for text content.\n    # Keep it conservative: escape & first, then < and >.\n    return (\n        s.replace(\"&\", \"&amp;\")\n        .replace(\"<\", \"&lt;\")\n        .replace(\">\", \"&gt;\")\n    )\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParaProcessResult:\n    xml: str\n    modified: bool\n    missing_keys: Tuple[str, ...]\n\n\ndef _replace_placeholders_in_text(text: str, mapping: Dict[str, object]) -> Tuple[str, Tuple[str, ...], bool]:\n    missing = []\n    replaced_any = False\n\n    def repl(m: re.Match) -> str:\n        nonlocal replaced_any\n        key = m.group(1)\n        if key in mapping:\n            replaced_any = True\n            val = mapping[key]\n            return \"\" if val is None else str(val)\n        missing.append(key)\n        return m.group(0)\n\n    new_text = PLACEHOLDER_RE.sub(repl, text)\n    return new_text, tuple(missing), replaced_any\n\n\ndef process_paragraph(para_xml: str, mapping: Dict[str, object]) -> ParaProcessResult:\n    \"\"\"Process a single <hp:p>...</hp:p> block.\n\n    Strategy:\n    - If intact {{key}} placeholders exist in the paragraph XML, do direct replacement.\n    - If braces exist but placeholders are split across multiple <hp:t> runs, merge the\n      paragraph's <hp:t> texts, replace on the combined text, then write it back into\n      the first <hp:t> and blank out the remaining <hp:t>.\n\n    If text was modified, remove any linesegarray element in that paragraph.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # Fast path: intact placeholders are present in the paragraph XML.\n    if PLACEHOLDER_RE.search(para_xml):\n        replaced, missing, modified = _replace_placeholders_in_text(para_xml, mapping)\n        if modified:\n            replaced = LINESEG_RE.sub(\"\", replaced)\n        return ParaProcessResult(xml=replaced, modified=modified, missing_keys=missing)\n\n    # If there are no braces, nothing to do.\n    if \"{{\" not in para_xml and \"}}\" not in para_xml:\n        return ParaProcessResult(xml=para_xml, modified=False, missing_keys=())\n\n    # Potential split placeholders: look inside <hp:t> runs.\n    t_parts = list(T_RE.finditer(para_xml))\n    if not t_parts:\n        # No <hp:t> segments found; nothing we can safely modify.\n        return ParaProcessResult(xml=para_xml, modified=False, missing_keys=())\n\n    combined_text = \"\".join(m.group(2) for m in t_parts)\n    if not PLACEHOLDER_RE.search(combined_text):\n        # Braces exist but still no placeholder pattern; leave as-is.\n        return ParaProcessResult(xml=para_xml, modified=False, missing_keys=())\n\n    replaced_text, missing, modified = _replace_placeholders_in_text(combined_text, mapping)\n    if not modified:\n        return ParaProcessResult(xml=para_xml, modified=False, missing_keys=missing)\n\n    # Write replaced combined text back into first <hp:t>, blank out the rest.\n    replaced_text_escaped = _xml_escape_text(replaced_text)\n\n    def _rewrite_t_runs(m: re.Match, _state={\"i\": 0}) -> str:\n        i = _state[\"i\"]\n        _state[\"i\"] = i + 1\n        if i == 0:\n            return f\"{m.group(1)}{replaced_text_escaped}{m.group(3)}\"\n        return f\"{m.group(1)}{''}{m.group(3)}\"\n\n    new_para = T_RE.sub(_rewrite_t_runs, para_xml)\n    new_para = LINESEG_RE.sub(\"\", new_para)\n    return ParaProcessResult(xml=new_para, modified=True, missing_keys=missing)\n\n\ndef process_xml(xml_text: str, mapping: Dict[str, object]) -> Tuple[str, int, Tuple[str, ...]]:\n    missing_all = []\n    modified_paras = 0\n\n    def para_cb(m: re.Match) -> str:\n        nonlocal modified_paras\n        para_xml = m.group(0)\n        res = process_paragraph(para_xml, mapping)\n        if res.missing_keys:\n            missing_all.extend(res.missing_keys)\n        if res.modified:\n            modified_paras += 1\n        return res.xml\n\n    new_xml, _ = PARA_RE.subn(para_cb, xml_text)\n\n    # As a conservative safety net: replace any remaining intact placeholders\n    # (e.g., if a placeholder was outside <hp:p> blocks).\n    if PLACEHOLDER_RE.search(new_xml):\n        new_xml2, missing2, _modified2 = _replace_placeholders_in_text(new_xml, mapping)\n        if missing2:\n            missing_all.extend(list(missing2))\n        new_xml = new_xml2\n\n    return new_xml, modified_paras, tuple(missing_all)\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    with open(DATA_PATH, \"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        mapping = json.load(f)\n\n    missing_keys_total = set()\n    modified_paras_total = 0\n\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(TEMPLATE_PATH, \"r\") as zin:\n        infos = zin.infolist()\n        with zipfile.ZipFile(OUT_PATH, \"w\") as zout:\n            for info in infos:\n                data = zin.read(info.filename)\n\n                out_data = data\n                if info.filename.lower().endswith(\".xml\"):\n                    try:\n                        xml_text = data.decode(\"utf-8\")\n                    except UnicodeDecodeError:\n                        xml_text = None\n\n                    if xml_text is not None and (\"{{\" in xml_text or \"}}\" in xml_text):\n                        new_xml, modified_paras, missing = process_xml(xml_text, mapping)\n                        modified_paras_total += modified_paras\n                        missing_keys_total.update(missing)\n                        out_data = new_xml.encode(\"utf-8\")\n\n                #
